LocalKummerPrimitiveTower:

```lean
import ArithmeticTeichmullerSpaces.Act1Scene1.LocalKummerPadicModule
import Mathlib.RingTheory.RootsOfUnity.AlgebraicallyClosed

/-!
source-id: JT-SCENE1-LOCAL-KUMMER-PRIMITIVE-TOWER
source: the compatible primitive roots underlying Z_p(1) in Joshi III (3190--3208).
fidelity: constructs a compatible sequence whose nth level has exact order p^n
in an algebraically closed characteristic-zero field. No compatible generator
is supplied by a caller.
boundary: Scene1/Derived
-/

namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalKummer.TateBasis
universe v
noncomputable section
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {Ω : Type v} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosed Ω]

def primitiveP : Ω := Classical.choose (HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_isPrimitiveRoot Ω p)

theorem primitiveP_spec : IsPrimitiveRoot (primitiveP (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) p :=
  Classical.choose_spec (HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_isPrimitiveRoot Ω p)

def positiveTower : RootTower (K := Ω) (Ω := Ω) p (primitiveP (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) :=
  rootTower p (Fact.out : p.Prime).pos _

def generatorValues : ℕ → Ω
  | 0 => 1
  | n + 1 => (positiveTower (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).root n

theorem generatorValues_isPrimitive (n : ℕ) :
    IsPrimitiveRoot (generatorValues (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n) (p ^ n) := by
  cases n with
  | zero => simpa only [generatorValues, pow_zero] using (IsPrimitiveRoot.one (M := Ω))
  | succ n =>
    apply IsPrimitiveRoot.iff_orderOf.mpr
    apply orderOf_eq_prime_pow
    · change (positiveTower (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).root n ^ (p ^ n) ≠ 1
      rw [RootTower.root_pow_eq, Algebra.algebraMap_self_apply]
      exact primitiveP_spec.ne_one (Fact.out : p.Prime).one_lt
    · change (positiveTower (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).root n ^ (p ^ (n + 1)) = 1
      rw [pow_succ, pow_mul, RootTower.root_pow_eq, Algebra.algebraMap_self_apply]
      exact primitiveP_spec.pow_eq_one

theorem generatorValues_succ_pow (n : ℕ) :
    generatorValues (p := p) (Ω := Ω) (n + 1) ^ p = generatorValues (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n := by
  cases n with
  | zero =>
    change (positiveTower (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).root 0 ^ p = 1
    rw [RootTower.root_zero, Algebra.algebraMap_self_apply]
    exact primitiveP_spec.pow_eq_one
  | succ n => exact (positiveTower (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).root_succ n

def generator : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω :=
  ⟨fun n => Units.mk0 (generatorValues n)
    ((generatorValues_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).isUnit
      (pow_pos (Fact.out : p.Prime).pos n).ne').ne_zero, by
    constructor
    · intro n
      apply Units.ext
      exact (generatorValues_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).pow_eq_one
    · intro n
      apply Units.ext
      exact generatorValues_succ_pow n⟩

theorem generator_isPrimitive (n : ℕ) :
    IsPrimitiveRoot ((generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).1 n) (p ^ n) :=
  IsPrimitiveRoot.coe_units_iff.mp (generatorValues_isPrimitive n)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalKummer.TateBasis
```

LocalKummerTateBasis:

```lean
import ArithmeticTeichmullerSpaces.Act1Scene1.LocalKummerPrimitiveTower
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Span.Basic
import Mathlib.NumberTheory.Padics.ProperSpace
import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Equiv.Basic
import Mathlib.Topology.Homeomorph.Lemmas

/-!
source-id: JT-SCENE1-LOCAL-KUMMER-TATE-BASIS
source: Z_p(1) as a topological rank-one Z_p module, Joshi III (3190--3208).
fidelity: constructs the unique p-adic exponent of every compatible root
sequence. Scalar multiplication by a primitive compatible sequence is a
continuous linear equivalence from Z_p to the actual Tate inverse limit.
boundary: Scene1/Derived
-/

namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalKummer.TateBasis
open scoped Topology
universe v
noncomputable section
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {Ω : Type v} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosed Ω]

local instance levelModNeZero (n : ℕ) : NeZero (p ^ n) :=
  ⟨(pow_pos (Fact.out : p.Prime).pos n).ne'⟩

def levelExponent (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) (n : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  Classical.choose ((generator_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).eq_pow_of_mem_rootsOfUnity
    ((mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mpr (x.2.1 n)))

theorem levelExponent_spec (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) (n : ℕ) :
    levelExponent x n < p ^ n ∧
      (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).1 n ^ levelExponent x n = x.1 n :=
  Classical.choose_spec ((generator_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).eq_pow_of_mem_rootsOfUnity
    ((mem_rootsOfUnity _ _).mpr (x.2.1 n)))

theorem levelExponent_compatible (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) (n : ℕ) :
    levelExponent x (n + 1) ≡ levelExponent x n [MOD p ^ n] := by
  have he : (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).1 n ^ levelExponent x (n + 1) =
      (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).1 n ^ levelExponent x n := by
    rw [(levelExponent_spec x n).2]
    rw [← (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)).2.2 n, ← pow_mul, Nat.mul_comm, pow_mul,
      (levelExponent_spec x (n + 1)).2, x.2.2 n]
  have hm := pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq.mp he
  rwa [← (generator_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).eq_orderOf] at hm

private theorem exponent_dvd (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) (n : ℕ) :
    (p : ℤ) ^ n ∣ (levelExponent x (n + 1) : ℤ) - levelExponent x n := by
  rw [← Int.natCast_pow, ← ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd]
  simp only [Int.cast_sub, Int.cast_natCast, sub_eq_zero]
  exact (ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff _ _ _).mpr (levelExponent_compatible x n)

def exponent (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) : ℤ_[p] :=
  PadicInt.ofIntSeq (fun n => (levelExponent x n : ℤ))
    (PadicInt.isCauSeq_padicNorm_of_pow_dvd_sub _ p (exponent_dvd x))

theorem exponent_toZModPow (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) (n : ℕ) :
    PadicInt.toZModPow n (exponent x) = (levelExponent x n : ZMod (p ^ n)) := by
  simpa only [exponent, Int.cast_natCast] using
    PadicInt.toZModPow_ofIntSeq_of_pow_dvd_sub (fun i => (levelExponent x i : ℤ))
      p (exponent_dvd x) n

theorem padicPower_exponent (x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) :
    padicPower (exponent x) (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) = x := by
  apply Subtype.ext
  funext n
  rw [padicPower_apply, exponent_toZModPow, ZMod.val_natCast,
    Nat.mod_eq_of_lt (levelExponent_spec x n).1, (levelExponent_spec x n).2]

theorem padicPower_generator_injective :
    Function.Injective (fun r : ℤ_[p] => padicPower r (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω))) := by
  intro r s hrs
  apply PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp
  intro n
  apply ZMod.val_injective
  apply (generator_isPrimitive (p := p) (Ω := Ω) n).pow_inj
    (ZMod.val_lt _) (ZMod.val_lt _)
  exact congrArg (fun x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω => x.1 n) hrs

def scalarMap : ℤ_[p] →ₗ[ℤ_[p]] Additive (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) :=
  LinearMap.toSpanSingleton ℤ_[p] _ (Additive.ofMul (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)))

theorem scalarMap_bijective : Function.Bijective (scalarMap (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) := by
  constructor
  · intro r s hrs
    apply padicPower_generator_injective (p := p) (Ω := Ω)
    exact congrArg Additive.toMul hrs
  · intro x
    exact ⟨exponent x.toMul, congrArg Additive.ofMul (padicPower_exponent x.toMul)⟩

def linearEquiv : ℤ_[p] ≃ₗ[ℤ_[p]] Additive (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) :=
  LinearEquiv.ofBijective scalarMap scalarMap_bijective

theorem continuous_scalarMap : Continuous (scalarMap (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) := by
  exact continuous_smul.comp
    (show Continuous (fun r : ℤ_[p] => (r, Additive.ofMul (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)))) from
      continuous_id.prodMk continuous_const)

local instance levelUnitsTopology : TopologicalSpace Ωˣ := ⊥
local instance levelUnitsDiscrete : DiscreteTopology Ωˣ := ⟨rfl⟩

instance tateT2Space : T2Space (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) :=
  (Topology.IsEmbedding.induced
    (Subtype.val_injective : Function.Injective (fun x : cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω => x.1))).t2Space

instance tateAdditiveT2Space : T2Space (Additive (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω)) :=
  T2Space.of_injective_continuous
    (f := fun x : Additive (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) => x.toMul)
    (fun _ _ h => congrArg Additive.ofMul h) continuous_toMul

def equiv : ℤ_[p] ≃L[ℤ_[p]] Additive (cyclotomicTateGroup p Ω) :=
  ContinuousLinearEquiv.ofIsHomeomorph linearEquiv
    (isHomeomorph_iff_continuous_bijective.mpr ⟨continuous_scalarMap, linearEquiv.bijective⟩)

theorem equiv_apply (r : ℤ_[p]) :
    equiv (p := p) (Ω := Ω) r = r • Additive.ofMul (generator (p := p) (Ω := Ω)) := rfl

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalKummer.TateBasis
```

IutInitialThetaTateCoordinates:

```lean
import ArithmeticTeichmullerSpaces.Act1Scene1.LocalKummerTateBasis
import ArithmeticTeichmullerSpaces.Act1Scene1.IutInitialThetaPadicKummer

/-!
source-id: JT-SCENE1-INITIAL-THETA-TATE-COORDINATES
source: Joshi III local Z_p(1)-valued Kummer class (3190--3208).
fidelity: supplies an actual topological Z_p basis at each local closure.
It gives continuous coordinates of the existing principal-unit cocycle and
proves reconstruction. These are coordinates, not a BK logarithm.
boundary: Scene1/Derived
-/

namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PrincipalUnitKummer
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped Topology
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

local instance tateCoordinateResiduePrime
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1).Prime := ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime s.1⟩

local instance tateCoordinateLocalCharZero
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    CharZero (localField D s) :=
  charZero_of_injective_ringHom (NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding s.1.maximalIdeal).injective

def tateBasis (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1] ≃L[ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1]]
      Additive (LocalKummer.cyclotomicTateGroup (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1) (closure D s)) :=
  LocalKummer.TateBasis.equiv

def cocycleCoordinate (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    C((closure D s ≃ₐ[localField D s] closure D s), ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1]) :=
  ⟨fun σ => (tateBasis D s).symm (Additive.ofMul (cocycle D s σ)),
    (tateBasis D s).symm.continuous.comp (continuous_ofMul.comp (continuous_cocycle D s))⟩

theorem cocycleCoordinate_reconstruct
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (σ : closure D s ≃ₐ[localField D s] closure D s) :
    tateBasis D s (cocycleCoordinate D s σ) = Additive.ofMul (cocycle D s σ) :=
  (tateBasis D s).apply_symm_apply _

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PrincipalUnitKummer
```

关键完整类型（ring/topology/group/field/algebra 实例上下文同生产文件）：

```lean
ContinuousH1Recognition.classOf_eq_zero_iff
  (X : TopRep.{v} R G) (f : C(G, X))
  (hf : ∀ g h, f (g * h) = X.ρ g (f h) + f g) :
  ContinuousH1Homogeneous.classOf X f hf = 0 ↔
    ∃ t : X, ∀ g, f g = X.ρ g t - t
LocalKummer.padicH1Equiv (p : ℕ) :
  LocalKummer.continuousH1 (K := K) (Ω := Ω) p ≃
    continuousCohomology 1 (LocalKummer.padicTateTopRep (K := K) (Ω := Ω) p)
PrincipalUnitKummer.padicClass_eq_zero_iff
  (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
  (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
  PrincipalUnitKummer.padicClass D s = 0 ↔
    ∃ t : LocalKummer.cyclotomicTateGroup (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)
      (PrincipalUnitKummer.closure D s),
      ∀ σ : PrincipalUnitKummer.closure D s ≃ₐ[PrincipalUnitKummer.localField D s]
        PrincipalUnitKummer.closure D s, PrincipalUnitKummer.cocycle D s σ = σ • t / t
```

双射仅为现有无拓扑/模块结构的 explicit cocycle 商与标准 integral H¹ 之间
的集合 equivalence；不据此赋予旧商未经证明的拓扑或声称 ℤₚ→ℚₚ H¹ 单射。

```lean
    Nonempty (LocalKummer.RootTower (K := PrincipalUnitKummer.localField D s)
      (Ω := PrincipalUnitKummer.localField D s) (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)
      (PaperLogWitnessHull.principalUnit D s))
```

本单元不将“每层分别有根”直接等同“兼容根塔存在”；已证明的反向判据
要求同一个兼容 tower。无根非零推论保留明确的有限层无根条件，不能据此
声称 actual 非零已完成。integral→rational H¹ 核问题仍独立。

S1-BND-341 最终决定（2026-10-14）：proved / accepted，owner Scene1。

公理仅标准允许集合。integral 类零 iff 基域 compatible tower、实际适配
及有限层根必要条件闭合，Prologue 仍 R5，Core 假设/退休/迁移增量全为 0。
未给出 actual 无根见证或排除 compatible tower，故 actual integral/rational
类非零、H¹ₑ/BK 及 theta/untilt/volume 联合模型保持 open；G1–G4 无闸门
关闭。集合双射、系数映射核和每层非相容选根继续分别处理，资源仅记总览。

## S1-BND-406：actual Haar 成本生产者与 VolumeTransfer 契约修复

2026-10-16，Prologue-R5→R5；derive；owner Scene1。

| 字段 | 本次记录 |
| --- | --- |
| source-id | `JT-SCENE1-ACTUAL-HULL-COST-TRANSFER` |
| declaration / statement | `ActualHullCostTransfer.link.logVolume = -Real.log(actualVolume)`（定义）；`volumeTransfer : VolumeTransfer link`；`packetCollationHull : PacketCollationHull link`；`volume_eq_exp_neg_logVolume : actualVolume = Real.exp (-link.logVolume)` |
| target-role / source | 将 S1.321 的实际 signed Haar 转移转换为 S1.322 的非负成本，接入 S1.358 的 raw 次数会计消费者 |
| model-evidence | 每项 packetWeight 是实际 local Haar negative-log cost；仅保留严格正项，证明总和不变；正长度 actual ordinary collation 的 packet carrier 非空；每个 packet 已包含其 collated hull 成本，独立 hull charge 为空 |
| dependency-effect | 新增低扇出 Scene1 成本适配模块；ordinary-log collation 自行提供几何 certificates；`actualCostLink_volumeNormalization` 与 `actualCostLink_transferLogBound` 消费同一实际成本 link，归一化不再是自由输入 |
| circularity / minimality | 成本转移由实际 Haar 求和证明，不假设 transfer bound 或 3.12；theta/period/untilt 联合模型、论文 volume 身份仍 open |
| burden-delta | 减少有限成本转移和普通 log collation 几何输入负担；Core 假设/退休/转入/取出均 0；最终闸门仍 0/4 |
| proof-status / decision | proved / accepted；1173 checks、970 axiom prints 与 Scene1 聚合通过；公理仅含 Lean 标准三项；旧公开签名保持不变 |

纠正 S1.405 的推论范围：`log(actualVolume) < 0`，而 `-log(actualVolume) > 0`
（正 procession length）。旧审计只排除 signed-log identity，不能排除成本路线，
更不能据此判断作者错误或要求停工。S1.403 只说明参考工程没有可直接移植的
H¹ₑ/BK 实现，S1.404 只重申已存在的信任规则；两者都不禁止独立形式化。

补正 S1.402 验收记录：实际源码含 1161 条 check、958 条 axiom print。
先前遗漏的 `smul_zsmul` 失败耗时 191.957 秒；加上第二次失败 186.946 秒、
成功叶构建 196.309 秒及完整审计 152.127 秒，累计应为 727.339 秒。

## S1-BND-456：实际 q-root Tate-log 连续性消费者

2026-10-17，Prologue-R5→R5；derive；owner Scene1。

`qrootTateLog_continuous` 将通用 `KummerValuation.tateLog_continuous` 应用于实际 contracting q-root，消费既有非单位证书和原 rational H¹ 的有限维 Hausdorff 拓扑。首次默认 `maxHeartbeats 200000` 在 `isDefEq` 超时（350.30s，峰值 RSS 8.76 GiB）；按既定顺序第二次将该定理提高到 `1000000` 后通过（399.89s，峰值 RSS 8.76 GiB），未触发进一步优化/翻倍。该声明仅提供实际 Tate-log 的连续性，不识别 H¹ₑ/BK、crystalline、period、different untilt 或 paper `thetaVolume`。

本轮收尾验证：直接热编译 `Axioms.SceneBounds.lean` 与 `Act1Scene1.lean` 均通过，分别耗时 138.34s 和 129.26s；CPU affinity `0-13`、Lake `LEAN_NUM_THREADS=10`、Lean `-j1`，峰值 RSS 分别约 7.95 GiB 与 7.91 GiB，未触及 85 GiB 停止阈值。两入口输出的新声明仅依赖 `propext`、`Classical.choice`、`Quot.sound`，无 `sorryAx`。`Checks.SceneBounds` 已在同一轮叶模块刷新后通过；未使用 `lake build`，未重编译 Mathlib。按 maxHeartbeats 规则，第二次失败先做了针对性拆分和显式中间事实，再将该叶模块上限设为 `2000000`；轻量非零结论使用 `1000000` 通过。

## S1-BND-457：实际 q-root Tate-log 通用桥接与分支公式负面证据

2026-10-17，Prologue-R5→R5；derive / negative evidence；owner Scene1。

`qrootTateLog_as_generic` 以定义等式暴露实际 `qrootTateLog` 到通用 `KummerValuation.tateLog` 的实例，编译上限按前两次失败后的规则保留为 `4000000`。尝试把完整实际对象直接包装成 `qrootTateLog_eq_ambientLog_sub` 时，默认 `200000` 在 `isDefEq` 超时；提高到 `1000000` 后仍超时；抽出局部 `q`/`hq` 并翻倍到 `2000000` 后仍在 `whnf` 超时；再抽出 `qrootTateLog_as_generic` 并翻倍到 `4000000` 后仍在 `whnf` 超时。该高展开 wrapper 已撤回，未进入公开接口；通用分支定理和实际桥接保持可消费。失败尝试未改变边界，也未重编译 Mathlib。
